namespace = cosmo_menaharemestate_events

#upgrade palace
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremVisit	
			value = 1
			}
		}
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_DANCER_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremVisit
			value = 21
			}
			}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1C_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremVisit
			value = 41
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1D_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremVisit
			value = 61
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1E_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremVisit
			value = 81
			}
		}
	}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1F_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremVisit
			value = 101
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1G_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremVisit
			value = 121
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1H_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremVisit
			value = 141
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1B_eventPicture
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}	


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_1	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_1_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_2	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_2_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_3	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_3_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_4	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_4_upgrade
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_5	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_5_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_6	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_6_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_7	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_7_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_8	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_8_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremUpgrade
		value = 1
		}
		}								
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_1_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_2_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_3_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_4_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_5_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_6_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_7_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_8_upgrade	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_all_upgrade
		}
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_1
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_2
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_3
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_4
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_5
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_6
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_7
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_8						
		}					
}

#Start event from estate
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.10
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.10.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.10.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
		has_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
	}
	has_ruler_flag = cosmo_harem_launch
		OR = {
			culture_group = byzantine
			culture_group = caucasian
			culture_group = turko_semitic
			culture_group = maghrebi
			culture_group = iranian
			culture_group = tartar
		}
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_harem_launch
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_harem_ruler_visit
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_mil
	
				}																				
			}
		}		
		}
		}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.10.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}	
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.30
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}	
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.10100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_piety_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_advisors_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_onsenaffairs_events.401
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_renovate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1
			}
		}																										
	}					
}

#grant harem 1a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.11
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.11.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_1A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_1a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = moroccan
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = moroccan
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = moroccan
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_1a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = mena_concubine_1a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = moroccan
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = moroccan
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = moroccan
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_1a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.11.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = moroccan
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = moroccan
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_menaharem_1a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_menamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.11.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}						
		}					
}

#grant harem 1c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.13
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.13.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.13.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_1C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_1c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = berber
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = berber
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = berber
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_1c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = mena_concubine_1c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = berber
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = berber
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = berber
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_1c
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_1c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.13.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = berber
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = berber
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_menaharem_1c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_menamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.13.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650

		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_3a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hejazi_culture
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hejazi_culture
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = hejazi_culture
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_3a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = mena_concubine_3a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hejazi_culture
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hejazi_culture
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hejazi_culture
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_3a
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_3a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.31.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = hejazi_culture
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = hejazi_culture
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_menaharem_3a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_menamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.31.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

#grant harem 4b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.42
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.42.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.42.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_4B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_4b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = yemeni_culture
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = yemeni_culture
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = yemeni_culture
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_4b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = mena_concubine_4b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yemeni_culture
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}

				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}

		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = al_iraqiya_arabic
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = al_iraqiya_arabic
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_menaharem_5a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_menamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem

			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

		}
		}

#grant harem 5b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.52
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.52.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.52.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_5B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_5b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = turkish
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = turkish
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = turkish
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_5b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = mena_concubine_5b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = turkish
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = turkish
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = turkish
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_5b
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_5b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.52.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = turkish
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = turkish
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_menaharem_5b								
		country_event = {

			}
		tooltip = {

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA

#grant harem 6b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.62
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.62.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.62.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_6b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = georgian
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = georgian
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = georgian
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_6b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = mena_concubine_6b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = georgian
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = georgian
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = georgian
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_6b
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_6b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.62.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = georgian
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = georgian
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_menaharem_6b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_menamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.62.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs

country_event = {

	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.71.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.71.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_7A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_7a
		}
		}

			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = bashkir
				culture = nogaybak
				culture = astrakhani
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = bashkir
				culture = nogaybak
				culture = astrakhani

			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = bashkir
				primary_culture = nogaybak
				primary_culture = astrakhani					
				}
		}
			
	}

#			days = 30
#		}

		}

		clr_ruler_flag = bashkir_consort

		}

		clr_ruler_flag = nogaybak_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = bashkir_consort

		}

				culture = bashkir
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince

			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}

			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

				development = 15
			}

		}				
		}

#grant palace 7b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.72
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.72.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.72.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_7B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_7b
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = mishary
				culture = kazani
				culture = crimean
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = mishary
				culture = kazani
				culture = crimean

			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = mishary
				primary_culture = kazani
				primary_culture = crimean					
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_7b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = mena_concubine_7b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = mishary
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = mishary_consort

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kazani
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = kazani_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = mishary_consort

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = crimean
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = crimean_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = kazani_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = mishary_consort

		}												
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = mishary_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = mishary
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = mishary
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = mishary
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = kazani_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kazani
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kazani
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kazani
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = crimean_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = crimean
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = crimean
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = crimean
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}												
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_7b
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_7b_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.72.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
			OR = {
				primary_culture = mishary
				primary_culture = kazani
				primary_culture = crimean
			}		

					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = mishary_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = mishary
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = kazani_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = kazani
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = crimean_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = crimean
		}
		}												
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_7b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_menamarriage_events.20
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = oirats_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = uzbehk_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = turkmeni_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = khalkha_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = chahar_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = mongol_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = mishary_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = kazani_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = crimean_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.72.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = oirats_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = uzbehk_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = turkmeni_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = khalkha_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = chahar_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = mongol_consort				
		clr_ruler_flag = mishary_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = kazani_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = crimean_consort
		}

			duration = 3650
		}

#grant harem 8b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.82
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.82.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.82.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_8B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_8b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = kurdish
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = kurdish
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = kurdish
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_8b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = mena_concubine_8b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kurdish
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kurdish
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = kurdish
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_8b
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_8b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

#grant harem 9b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.92
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.92.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.92.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_9B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_9b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = persian
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = persian
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = persian
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_9b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = mena_concubine_9b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = persian
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = persian
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = persian
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_9b
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_9b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.92.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = persian
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = persian
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_menaharem_9b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_menamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.92.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_MENA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1003
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1003.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1003.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_10C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_10c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = baluchi
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = baluchi
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = baluchi
				}
		}

			}

			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = baluchi
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = baluchi
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}

#loop to begining
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.101
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.101.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.101.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_1_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_1_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_all_upgrade
			}
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_1a
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_1b
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_1c
			}
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_2_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_menu_all_upgrade
			}
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_2a
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_2b
				has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_2c
			}

			}

					}
				}

			country_event = {

			}

			if = {
				limit = {
			NOT = {

		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremSevenCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_indiaese_master_of_mint
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremSevenCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremSevenCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

		}

			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}

		set_ruler_flag = harem_kyushuan_navigator
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremEightCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremEightCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}

			value = 280
			}

			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}

		set_ruler_flag = harem_togoku_colonial_governor
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremNineCCounter	
			value = 281
			}
			}

		which = cosmoMHaremNineCCounter
		value = 0
			}

			has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_10c
			}
					save_event_target_as = consortprovince		
		}																
		}
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1030.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoMENACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremTenCCounter
		value = 3
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremTenCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_togoku_diplomat
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremTenCCounter	
			value = 281
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremTenCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

		}
		}

		}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1200
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1200.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1200.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_12_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

		immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		#immediate for palace estate				
		}
		}

		option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1200.a				
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_12a
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_12a
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1210
			}
		}
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1200.b
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {

		}

			}

	}

	hidden_effect = {

			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = mena_concubine_12a
			}
					save_event_target_as = consortprovince		
		}																
		}
	}		
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.1210.a	   
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoMENACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremTwelveACounter
		value = 3
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremTwelveACounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_indiaese_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoMHaremTwelveACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoMHaremTwelveACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

#grant harem cheat
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.999
	title = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.999.t
	desc = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.999.d
	picture = cosmogfx_HAREM_MENA_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no			
	}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_menaharemestate_events.999.a
		capital_scope = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_1a_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_1b
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_1b_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_1c
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_1c_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_2a
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_2a_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_2b
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_2b_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_2c
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_2c_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_3a
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_3a_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_3b
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_3b_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = mena_concubine_3c
				duration =  -1
				desc = mena_concubine_3c_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = {
